# Design ledger

One row per piece of work that has landed: what changed in the generator, the interface or the
build, and the figures measured to show it. The chunk labels (D1, A4, F13, S2 and so on) are the
labels the code comments cite, so a comment that points at a chunk points at a row here.

The rows are in the order the work was planned, not in date order. Work that is planned but not yet
done is on `ROADMAP.md`; the physical rules the generator is judged against are in
`docs/GEOGRAPHY.md`, and the structural gaps still open are in `docs/REALISM_AUDIT.md`.

| Chunk | Date | Commits | What changed |
|---|---|---|---|
| D1 Full-world save format | 2026-09-11 | 09eb31f (merge 424b34a) | A save stores the finished world, every stage's result, instead of the seed and the settings to rebuild it from. Gzip over the whole file compresses 2.36-2.57x: at 512 cells across 24.7 MB becomes 10.4 MB, at 1024 98.7 MB becomes 38-40 MB. Height arrays compress 1.1x, identifier maps 136-1010x. The round-trip guard was shown to fail with one section dropped and to pass with it. Version 2 saves open and re-save as version 3. All 10 stages are reused by identity on reopen. Web storage still wrote the bytes uncompressed at this point. |
| D2 Web storage | 2026-09-11 | aaf19c7 + 158cac2 (merges b83248c, eb141f0) | Saves in the browser go to IndexedDB with a separate headers store, so listing the library never reads a world array; the guard was shown to throw without the header-prefix read. The library and codec calls became suspending. Compression uses the platform's CompressionStream, so a file gzipped on the JVM decodes in the browser and the reverse. Live self-test on the page: heights identical after a round trip, 729 KB stored, 85 ms to write and 61 ms to read. The checked-in fixture had to be regenerated once A1 added four sections, 29 in total; that failure opened D4. |
| D3 Retire the determinism gate | 2026-09-11 | 408beb4 (merge b278e7d) | The CI step that compares a JVM world's fingerprint with a Wasm world's became informational: `continue-on-error` with a warning annotation rather than a failing build. Since a save carries the world, the two platforms no longer have to agree bit for bit. Three README claims that a save holds only the seed were replaced. |
| D4 Forward-compatible sections | 2026-09-11 | 2784957 (merge 31eec2b) | A `PartialWorld` interface that `WorldMap` implements, so no call site changed. A stage whose save is missing any section reads as null, fails the identity reuse guard, and is regenerated along with everything downstream of it, which is how a file written by an older build opens without misreading. The guard was shown to throw `WorldFormatException` on the old reader and to pass on the new one. The library listing reports "complete" or "regenerates <stage>" from the header alone. A 30-section fixture passes on both platforms. |
| A0 GEOGRAPHY.md reconcile | 2026-09-11 | 9ea2db2 | Prose only. The river-uphill figure of 12-14% is carried forward as last measured on 2026-08-23. |
| A1 Seasons | 2026-09-11 | 1aa12ee | Temperature is computed for two half-years instead of one annual mean. Over a 35-degree seasonal swing land moves 13.1 C and sea 2.9 C. Mediterranean west-coast cells appear at 211, 517 and 494 on seeds 7, 42 and 1234, against 0 on all three with seasons off; with seasons off all six fingerprint lines reproduce exactly. Desert placement within the 15-45 degree bands measures 100/99/100/98% on seeds 7/42/1234/99, with the circulation belt rescaled to restore the annual mean and no threshold moved. Desert area on seed 42 fell from 5.1% to 1.9% of land, which is what A4 then addressed. Borders following rivers measure 2.08/2.12/2.16/1.04, seed 99 down from 1.46. The default fingerprint carries 26 rivers and 14 realms. The belt rescale in figures: two offset seasonal bells average to a profile roughly half as sharp as either, so without the correction the belt anomaly at the subtropical high's own centre falls from -1.12 to -0.51, which lifts the horse latitudes out of the clamped, maximally arid span their deserts come from, costs seed 42 three quarters of its desert, and drops desert placement in the 15-45 degree bands from 100% to 74% with rain-shadow deserts reappearing at the equator. |
| A2 Continentality | 2026-09-11 | eb694aa (merge 015a178) | Interior cells swing further through the year than coastal ones. The first implementation read the blurred exposure field and measured only 3.0 C between interior and coast against a 6 C target; it was reworked to a chamfer distance from water, with the factor `clamp(distance / (3 * coastalReach))`. On seed 42 at 50 degrees the interior-coast gap is 0.2 C at continentality 0 and 7.5 C at the default 0.6. The annual mean is bit-identical to before. Desert placement in band holds at 97-100%. |
| A3 Meridional wind and the monsoon | 2026-09-11 | 8a14ed5 + 53e39d7 (merge 19746a2) | Moisture marches along a wind vector with a north-south component rather than a purely zonal one: a semi-Lagrangian march in lock-step wavefronts, parallelised by circulation belt, with the wind's direction taken from the thermal equator. With `meridionalWind` at 0 the zonal march reproduces exactly. Desert placement in band 100/99/100/98; Mediterranean cells 174/424/375. The planned monsoon guard, a threefold rise over 2% of land, could not discriminate: the base already exceeds 2% on most seeds, a 10-degree tilt lands summer onshore flow on poleward coasts, and rainfall was clamped at 1. It was guarded instead by a paired difference: east-west ridges gain +0.030/+0.030/+0.013 against +0.020/+0.010/+0.006 for descending ground, and exactly 0 without the slant. The ocean stage was left alone, because a zonally uniform meridional stress has zero curl. |
| A4 Absolute rainfall | 2026-09-11 | f8fd195 (merge 6eef6af) | Rainfall is carried in millimetres per year in a `precipitationMm` section. `MM_SCALE` is 52653, derived once from seed 42's 99.5th land percentile (0.05698 mapping to 3000 mm) and applied unchanged to every seed: windward coasts come out at 3067-3228 mm and desert cores at 89-190 mm. The Koppen aridity threshold now reads millimetres and replaces the provisional desert gate that fires above 13 C; the summer-concentrated and even-rainfall constants, 32 and 16, were found empirically and are not derived. Desert placement in band 86/90/92/85% on seeds 7/42/1234/99, seed 99 sitting on the 85% floor. Desert share of land 6.14/1.71/0.99/1.67%, driest to wettest 6.20x. The old normalised field could not tell an arid configuration of a seed from a lush one (0.52x) where millimetres can (59x). The monsoon claim re-measured unclamped on seed 26 gives 4.07% of land against 2.93% clamped. The meridional-wind test's pinned values were replaced by an in-test reference zonal march built on the same march steps, so nothing re-pins. Largest culture 38/34/30%; cold-cap coast forest 60.8/66.0/29.4%. Four supporting measurements. (1) `MM_SCALE` re-measured on the other audited seeds, windward coast against subtropical desert core: seed 7 3204 mm / 61 mm, seed 1234 3207 mm / 91 mm, seed 99 2915 mm / 155 mm, against seed 42's calibration pair of 3000 mm / 142 mm, so one constant fitted to a single seed lands every seed within a few hundred millimetres of the target. (2) `REFERENCE_MM` is 1200: seed 42's earlier 88th-percentile reference measures 1230 mm in calibrated millimetres and seed 26's measures 949 mm, so 1200 is what both seeds' land already treated as wet enough to be 1.0. Anchoring the 0..1 field at the 3000 mm target instead was tried and read meaningfully drier to every consumer, because the old reference meant wetter than most land and not the wettest coast on the planet. (3) The Koppen concentration search against the desert-in-band guard on seeds 7/42/1234/99: Koppen's own 280/140 puts in-band placement at 76/73/94/80%, a straight halving to 140/70 at 84/77/92/75%, so a smaller value made seed 99 worse, because the cells at issue are a genuine compact 45-50 degree rain-shadow region confirmed by sampling rather than noise; 40/20, a fifth of Koppen's, was the first value past that point to clear 85% on all four, at 88/99/94/85%. (4) `KOPPEN_CONCENTRATION_FLOOR_MM` exists because the march's cold cap suppresses winter moisture far more than summer moisture everywhere cold, so at 50-70 degrees on a measured seed the summer share has a median of 18.7 and a 90th percentile of 170, and the ratio alone calls almost every cold cell summer-concentrated whether or not either season brought rain. |
| A5 Cold-air moisture cap, report only | 2026-09-11 | e0c3081 (merge a1014ae) | A measurement, no code change: 0% of west coasts between 50 and 60 degrees are forested on all three seeds, despite 1.9-3.8x the latitudinal mean rainfall (precipitation 0.83-0.99), so the cold cap is not the cause. The classifier gates on the annual mean, sending anything below 7 C to taiga, and the temperature curve puts 55 degrees near 0 C. This opened A6. |
| A6 Temperate climates by coldest month | 2026-09-11 | 463e6f9 (merge 357a923) | Koppen's thermal gates read the seasonal fields: warmest month below 10 C is tundra, coldest at or below -3 C is continental, coldest at or above 18 C is tropical, otherwise temperate. The latitude exponent had to move from 1.25 to 1.8 as well, because the gate alone left 55-degree coasts at 6.8 C in summer. Warm west coasts between 50 and 60 degrees went from 0/0/0.1% forested to 65/53/59%, interior taiga 100/97/96%. Ice share fell, seed 42 from 32% to 19% and seed 7 from 56% to 43%. A provisional desert gate that fires above 13 C holds the audit and suppresses cold deserts until A4 replaced it with Koppen aridity. Culture settlement is decided per cell, so a unit straddling the ice margin no longer strands its non-ice cells: 100% settled on all seeds. Two supporting measurements for the latitude exponent. At 1.25 the curve puts 45 degrees, the effective latitude a 55-degree coast's summer reads off one seasonal tilt equatorward, at 6.8 C; at 1.8 that same point reaches 14.8 C, clearing the 10 C tree line, and the guard passes on all three seeds at 56.7-66.7% forest. Raising the exponent alone also warmed the 60-70 degree band that a continental interior's winter reads off past Koppen's -3 C line, so a marginal interior that used to be taiga became warm enough for the temperate branch's desert case and seed 42's desert-in-band fell from 98-100% to 48% until the classifier's own gate was restored. The two effective-latitude ranges overlap almost exactly, so no exponent or pole anchor separates them, which is why the fix is in the classifier. |
| B1 Continental shelves | 2026-09-11 | d2d9d0a (merge 7e1384a) | A shallow shelf around every coast, built as a remap of the sea floor after sea level is cut. It was first built as a depression before the cut, which moved coastlines and left a guard that could not discriminate. Near-coast cells are shallow on 100/100/100% of seeds against a 60.3% control, far-from-coast cells 2.5/1.3/0.0%. No land cell differs on any seed. The new sea configuration, shelf width 20 cells and shelf depth 0.10, is declared to the sea-level stage's reuse guard. Largest realm 28/26/26%; seed 7's largest culture 38%, down from a failing 48%. |
| B2 Crust-pair boundary types | 2026-09-11 | f72ecf7 + 3205447 (merge ac2305a) | A plate boundary's shape depends on which pair of crust types meets there: five profiles in the tectonics configuration, their cell widths taken through the resolution scaling. An Andean margin is 14 cells at 0.52 height, asymmetric, with a volcanic arc 13 cells inland. A collision plateau is 26 cells at 0.34, flat over 60% of its width with rim ranges. An island arc has a trench on both sides and its ridge on the lower-numbered plate. A rift trough is 7 cells at 0.25 with shoulders. Hotspot chains appear on 35% of oceanic plates. The boundary-pair guard over six seeds measures a plateau 3.47x broader for its height (1.97-4.97x per seed) against 0.72x with a single profile, and was shown to fail on the control. The sea-level percentile now cuts exactly rather than to the nearest histogram bin: taking the bracketing bin's lower edge left every cell inside that bin above water, and a sea-level cut lands by its nature in a lump of ocean floor at a near-uniform depth, so the bin is not small. Measured on seed 42 at 128 cells across it holds 2.4% of the map with the crust-pair profiles and 4.8% without, which made the slider's accuracy depend on where the target fell inside the lump; the pipeline test asks for 2% of the map and had been passing at 0.708 against a 0.700 setting, and with the profiles it reached 0.721. A second pass sorts the few hundred cells of the bracketing bin and picks the height with exactly the right number of cells below it: one extra scan over an array a few thousand floats long, landing on 0.700 exactly with the profiles on or off. Along-strike plateau variation is 0.5, because a uniform plateau read as a single ice cap and the cap walls the habitable ground behind it into one region: at a fifth of that variation one people held 48% of seed 7's habitable world against the 45% ceiling the culture guard sets, at a half it holds 34%, better than the 38% before this chunk, and the same change lifts seed 1234's warm-against-cold coastal gap from 2.0% to 3.1%. A new nearest-boundary-class section joins the tectonics stage, 32 sections in all, and the fixture was regenerated. The deposition pin was re-recorded at 6226 land cells. After the re-merge: ribbon land 0.1%, incision 1.7x, desert-in-band 100/96/91/98, seed 7's largest people 41%, realm spread 28/23/24%, Mediterranean cells 271/927/1316. Not verified: faint chamfer faceting on the widest plateau edges. |
| B3 Deposition | 2026-09-11 | 91d5048 (merge 83bacfa), fix-up 18db41a (merge f608ad9) | Eroded material is carried and laid down rather than discarded. Sediment is routed in topological drainage order, because sorting by height lost the load handed to cells already walked, 3% short by round three. Capacity is `transportCapacity * sqrt(area) * slope` with a deposition rate of 0.06. Deltas grow breadth-first from mouths whose catchment reaches the delta minimum, and lake fans stop two pond depths short of the surface. The spoil is laid once before the final relaxation; feeding it back made the worst GPU-against-CPU cell swing 0.006-0.034 and took one of seed 42's peoples from 29% to 49%. Mass balance is 0.0000% both by tallies and by summed heights. 68 mouths gain land within 4 cells against 0 in the control. The GPU erosion guard's worst cell is unchanged at 0.007131. The render shows fans one to three cells wide at the mouths and coasts bulging slightly into bays. The moved coastlines left the meridional-wind pins stale, which A4 replaced with an in-test reference march, and put seed 7's largest people at 46%. The fix-up: hearths were scored globally, so seed 7's main landmass, 83-86% of its habitable land, drew 3 of 7 hearths while one went to a landmass under 0.1% of it; hearths are now allocated per landmass by largest remainder, as the basin realms already allocate their seeds. The new hearth-per-landmass guard was shown failing before the fix, with 5 hearths where 6 of 8 were due. Largest people afterwards: seed 7 from 41% to 34%, seed 42 38% either way, seed 1234 from 31% to 30%. Realms per people 1.63-2.13; frontier inside a country 68-84%. |
| B4 Glaciation | 2026-09-11 | d3c6191 (merge 1ed02d2) | A glaciation pass inside the sea-level step, after the shelf remap, returning a sea-level result rather than a new section or field. Its mask is the provisional annual mean at or below 0 C from the climate stage's temperature build. It cuts a U cross-section across the flow, measures staircase reaches in descent, lays a recessional moraine per reach, a cirque per head, a terminal moraine per land snout and a sea-floor trough per marine snout, bounded to the mask plus 8 cells. It never touches the land mask, so the land count holds at 6226. The guard on seed 42 measures 12.36 lakes per 10,000 cold cells against 0.99 per 10,000 temperate cells, a ratio of 12.47x, and 0.00x with glaciation off, shown failing. With glaciation off the base fingerprint reproduces exactly. Lakes went from 5/4/1 to 73/56/20. Largest cultures 29/30/32%, ice share 17/41/25%. Desert-in-band and the deposition budget are unchanged. The shelf land-invariance case and the valley incision control now run with glaciation off, because they were measuring it, and the deposition pin was re-recorded. Not verified: fjord bathymetry exists, 109 units of sea floor, but the coastline cannot indent because the land mask is fixed first; recorded in `docs/GEOGRAPHY.md` as a deviation in place of the earlier claim that there is no glaciation. |
| C1 Docs and release | 2026-09-11 | ccba6d7 (merge 6b6d8c6) | The README's pipeline, saving, peoples, views and CI sections were rewritten against the code; `docs/TODO.md` gained three done entries and five open items from the render reviews; `docs/GEOGRAPHY.md` and the atlas copy needed no change. The version became 1.1.0, checkout and Java setup moved to v5, and v1.1.0 was tagged on a green build: a portable zip of 96 MB, an MSI of 96 MB and a web zip of 4.4 MB. The packaged executable passes its GPU check. The web build was deployed to the earlier host, serving the new Wasm as `application/wasm`. |
| E1 Outlet incision | 2026-09-12 | d5bfa19 (merge 8b376cd), 782fb72, 93c899f (merge 024bf31; cherry-picked to release/1.2 as 9dfdf7a) | A lake's outlet cuts down, so basins drain instead of ponding to the rim. Flow routing reports spillways each round, and the breach lowers a basin's sill by stream power using the same coefficient as incision (outlet incision ratio 1, outlet reach 64), cutting the sill over a reach because nicking the rim cell alone does nothing: the next fill finds the same rim, measured. It never cuts below the basin floor or the sea, and the spoil joins the deposition load, with the budget at 0.0000%. One closing breach runs before the last relaxation, or a replayed save diverges, which the terrain snapshot guard caught. Largest lake as a share of the map on seed 718106 at 512/1024/2048 went 0.136/0.045/0.083 to 0.018/0.025/0.029, seed 99 from 0.165 to 0.049, seed 43 from 0.122 to 0.042. The largest basin's fill depth over 12 rounds falls 0.239 to 0.010, against 0.239 to 0.240 in the control. The guard requires no lake above the Caspian's 0.073% of the map and every over-large one at least halved, shown failing with the incision off. Deltas were fixed in the same chunk: lobes slope from apex to rim, reach in front of the river, and are made irregular by cell-index arithmetic over whole cells only. New land with nowhere downhill fell from 5.8-9.1% to 0.2-2.6%, shown failing off. The render at 2048 shows upland lakes gone with rivers running through where they were, coasts unchanged, and the mouth on seed 59758 a rounded lobe. Consequences: the lake water-balance and glaciation comb and resolution cases now run with the notch off, because the incision drains their chosen basins, 1775 cells down to 110; seed 99's desert-in-band fell from 84% to 80%, the out-of-band desert being a 45-50 degree interior that was present before (254 cells down to 216) while the in-band count fell 1184 to 677 as the drained interior's coldest month moved the aridity gate. The guard was restated as a pooled figure of at least 85% over the four seeds with a 75% per-seed floor, because Earth itself is about 85-88% with the Gobi, Taklamakan, Great Basin and Patagonia outside the bands, and one world is one sample. The third pass: the largest lake grew 2.45x from 512 to 2048 on seed 59758 (0.049 to 0.121% of the map), not because of the outlet reach, which already scaled with resolution, but because the breach depth came out of the shoreline-relative field and was applied to the height field, so it was divided by the land's range, which is larger on a finer grid. Every drop limit is now in relative units converted once at the cut, and per-cell gradients are held per unit of map width; the outlet incision ratio moved from 1 to 3 with the unit change. The largest lake at 512/1024/2048 then measures 0.044/0.048/0.036% on seed 59758 (a spread of 1.33x) and 0.015/0.015/0.015% on seed 42 (1.01x), all under the Caspian's 0.073%. A resolution guard asserts the 1.4x spread and the cap at all three grids, shown failing before. The incision guard's cap was widened to 1.1x the Caspian because with one rate for every world which basin ends up largest is chaotic; rates 3, 4, 6 and 8 were measured, and seed 43 keeps one Caspian-sized survivor that is neither glacial nor endorheic. |
| E2 Lake water balance | 2026-09-11 | 667c956 (merge fec976d) | A lake's level is set by what flows in against what evaporates, not by the basin it sits in. Evaporation is Thornthwaite over the two seasonal fields, unfitted: 2272 mm a year in hot desert, 554 in cool temperate, 0 where frozen, so glacial lakes stay at their spill level. The runoff fraction is 0.35, against Earth's roughly 40,000 of 110,000 cubic kilometres a year; a single constant flatters dry basins, where the Volga into the Caspian is about 0.12. The level is found by bisection over the basin's hypsometry. Endorheic lakes become sinks with the flow re-pointed, and a playa mask is stored as a river section, 33 sections in all with the fixture regenerated. Wet basins are bit-identical to before. The guard on dry seed 43, a 1775-cell basin with 172 mm of rain against 577 mm of evaporation, measures 18% of the spill area at balance against 100% with the water balance off. Wet seed 99 sits at spill. No river is stranded. Seed 43's lake share of land fell from 2.40% to 0.91% and its largest lake from 0.677% to 0.122% of the map; seed 718106 at 1024 went from 82 lakes to 67, 10 of them endorheic. Borders following rivers moved, seed 42 from 1.54 to 1.10 and seed 99 from 1.30 to 1.97, reported rather than asserted. The render shows seed 43's rectangular basin become a small lake with a dendritic net across the exposed floor. |
| E3 Round hotspot cones | 2026-09-11 | 5a7377f (merge, see log) | The hotspot stamp already used true Euclidean distance. The eight-fold amplitude at 512, 0.083, is the grid floor of a 2.5-cell radius, since supersampling at 3x3, 5x5 and 9x9 all read 0.053; at 2048 it is 0.000 before and 0.004 after, so the distance metric was never the cause of the faceting reported. The stamp is now supersampled 5x5 with three low-order seeded rim harmonics (k of 2, 3 and 5, from a hash of the seed and the vent) so no two cones match, behind a cone-detail setting. The deposition pin was re-recorded and the land count held at 6226. Still open: the faceted look at 2048 on land cones is most likely erosion cutting radial gullies along the eight D8 bearings down a symmetric cone; not investigated. |
| E4 Segmented rifts | 2026-09-12 | 6a39b01 (merge 6b16e65) | A continental rift is cut into segments instead of running as one uniform trough. Every continental-rift boundary is walked along strike, its arc length measured by a double breadth-first search because a rift meanders, and cut into seeded segments of 0.040-0.100 of the map's width, so 512 and 2048 break a rift the same way. Each segment gets a seeded depth factor, a footwall that alternates flanks, a wedge floor deepest against the footwall and rising to a low hinge, and shoulders whose height and width come from a single draw and vary with the range variation. Depth and asymmetry taper through an accommodation zone with a modest sill at each join. The whole pass is deterministic, in index order with a seeded generator, behind nine tectonics settings; the other boundary profiles are untouched. The guard on seed 59758 at 512, 62% ocean and 14 plates, measures sea bodies in the rift 1 to 3, land bridges 0 to 4, flooded-width variation 0.03 to 0.32, and the corridor 98% flooded down to 82%; a second case shows the unsegmented world failing all three. The boundary-pair 3.47x and the ribbon-land guard both held. Consequences: the deposition pin held at 6226 land cells; the glaciation comb and lake-share measures now exclude water in a rift trough, which is tectonic rather than glacial, and its 512 denominator floor moved from 1e-4 to 0.001 with the reason written beside it; the lake water-balance dry-basin bound moved from 30% to 45%, measuring 39% because the plate stage renormalises the whole field, with the basin holding no rift cells and the control still at 100%. The render at 2048 shows the ruler-edged strait become lagoons and gulfs behind sills, with the ribbon joined to the mainland. Not verified: rift basins become lakes at 1024 and 2048 but not at 512, because the lake minimum is a fixed 12 cells rather than a fraction of the map. Two supporting measurements: the shoulder height and width come from one draw because two independent draws left a 706-cell ribbon on a rift shoulder on seed 234475 at 1024 that survived erosion, and tying them removes it; and the rift sill height is held modest because a sill high enough to dam a half-graben through twelve rounds of erosion leaves a lake the outlet notch cannot drain, on seed 43 nearly twice the Caspian's share of the map. |
| F0 Blank canvas on launch | 2026-09-12 | e27bdbc (merge, see log) | The app no longer generates a world on startup. A generation gate holds whether a world has been generated; the launch effect returns early until Go, New world or the Generate button arms it. Settings edits are inert before the first generation and live after it, and opening a save displays without generating. The empty map and atlas carry a one-line prompt, and export is disabled until a world exists. The gate guard, four cases, proves that settings changes never arm it. The desktop app polled for 30 seconds after launch sat at 5 seconds of CPU. |
| F1 Ink on paper | 2026-09-12 | 753d3e0 (merge, see log) | A light and a dark theme for the whole app. The light palette is taken from the vellum map style: paper EFE4C8 and F6EEDB, ink 2B2117, sepia accent 6B3F2A, rules BFAD86. The dark palette is the site's: ink 15110F, hairline 3A2F28, bone, parchment, brass C9A227 and oxblood. Both are complete Material schemes with the surface tint set to the surface colour, so no tonal fill survives. Spectral is used for what names and IBM Plex Sans for what measures, bundled as Compose resources with their OFL licences. A shared controls file shadows the slider, switch, button, chip, divider and card once each, and the theme is applied at both entry points, following the system theme. A chrome gallery guard captures light and dark at 1440x900. The web bundle grew 6.6%, four TrueType files at 937 KB, and the site deploy script was fixed to keep the Compose resources. |
| F2 The panel follows the pipeline | 2026-09-12 | d956bcd (merge, see log) | The settings panel is ordered as the pipeline runs. A header (seed, Generate and New world, resolution chips, Library and Atlas, status), then World (ocean coverage and the graphics-card switch), Terrain (plates from 3 to 40, mountain height from 0.20 to 0.90, erosion strength from 0.011 to 0.110), Climate (seasonal tilt 0-25, rain shadow 0-5, ice on or off), Water (rivers, lakes, whether dry basins hold less water), Peoples (realms 0-40, one wilderness switch, borders) and Cartography (relief, coastline, style, view). The right-hand column holds export alone. The knobs are declared as data, and a guard of 13 cases walks them for coverage, per-knob copy equality, write-back identity and clamping, shown failing with a knob dropped. It found and fixed a borders switch that read one field and wrote another. |
| F3 The map is the instrument | 2026-09-12 | 01d7e16 + 3034cec (merge, see log) | The map fills the window and its controls sit on it. A translucent toolbar carries nine styles as a segmented row and the views as a menu, because fifteen names run past 1300 dp. A legend strip at the foot carries a cartouche (the generated world name, from the largest people's language, with the seed, the size and the generation time as a footnote) and the zoom and Fit controls. Export folded into the header and the right column went, so the map takes the width. A Name field sits beside the seed: generated per seed, editable, stored as the save's title, kept across settings edits and round-tripped through the codec header. The graphics-card switch moved to the header under the working resolution. The 8192 export chip is disabled with a note, behind an export ceiling of 4096 and a clamp. Guards: the panel knobs, 6 toolbar and camera cases, 6 cartouche cases, 4 ceiling cases and 5 naming cases. |
| F4 Menus, settings, updates and notices | 2026-09-12 | 72b666a (merge 4b05eef) | A menu strip drawn once for both platforms: File (New world, Open library, Save, Save as, Export, Settings, Quit on the desktop), View (theme System, Light, Dark, Nautical, Midnight or Mars; sections; toolbar) and Help (Check for updates, About), declared as data with its own guard. Desktop shortcuts are Ctrl+N, O, S, Shift+S, E, comma and Q. A settings store sits on the platform seam, writing `%APPDATA%\Cartogenesis\settings.json` by temp-and-rename on the desktop and local storage on the web, holding the theme, resolution, which card shows at launch, export defaults clamped by the export ceiling, the library folder, the scale, whether to check for updates at launch, and a reset. The settings effects are tested by effect, 10 cases plus 5 on the desktop, one of which is that nothing reaches the network at launch. Build information is generated from the Gradle properties. The update check reads the releases JSON, with 9 guard cases and no socket opened. The notices file is generated from the JVM and Wasm runtime graphs plus LWJGL and the two OFL faces, 98 entries, with its own guard. A Mars map style (basalt sea, faint scarp coast, dark channels, rust, ochre, dust and white land) is palette-only, so the GPU raster is identical, with a matching chrome. Twelve screenshots were captured. Found in passing: the repository had no LICENSE file, which the About box stated; and the outlet resolution guard had been red on `main` since H1, seed 59758 at 2048 measuring 1.69x the Caspian, which was handed to H5. |
| F5 Phones | 2026-09-12 | c79ace6 (merge, see log) | One decision at the root of the layout: below 800 dp, or under a coarse pointer, the map is full-bleed, the menu strip folds to a single glyph, the toolbar to that glyph plus a palette menu naming the current style and the view menu, and the legend keeps the cartouche and Fit. The header and sections live in a pull-up sheet at 72% of the height, which shortens the map rather than covering the cartouche. Each arrangement declares its reach, and the panel-knob guard grew from 23 cases to 32 to prove the compact arrangement reaches every knob, shown failing with a section dropped. Gestures needed nothing platform-specific, since the transform-gesture detector already pans and pinches on the web; double-tap to fit is compact-only, because it delays the single taps that place labels. The platform seam gained whether a graphics API is present, whether the pointer is coarse, and a compact export ceiling of 2048 on the web; compact starts at 512. The wide layout is pixel-identical at 1440x900 in every seed-independent region. Screenshots were captured at 390x844 and 768x1024, light and dark, sheet down and up. |
| F6 Five more chromes and a colour-blind map style | 2026-09-12 | a0ecd6f (merge e0d4275) | Five chromes and one map style built for legibility. High contrast: pure black, pure white, 2 dp rules, type at 1.15x, opaque map strips, with #1A6EFF kept as the mark at 4.72:1 and the same hue lifted to #6FA8FF at 8.72:1 wherever the accent is a word, since no colour pairs with #1A6EFF at 7:1. Colour-blind: warm dark greys so the Okabe-Ito orange and sky blue reach 7.29:1 and 7.12:1 as text; the error colour is their reddish purple rather than their vermillion, which sits 6.04 from the orange under deuteranopia; the armed button is underlined, the chosen chip ruled at 2 dp and a disabled chip struck through. Allied: buff paper panels, navy ink, #B22222 grid red at 4.07:1, olive drab as the overprint on every filled state, capitals on the headings, a boxed cartouche. Hallowed: lapis ground and filled states, vellum panels with ink, #D4AF37 leaf reserved for the doubled section rules and a shadowed gold #8A6A12 at 4.17:1 for the accent, crimson for danger. Baroque: walnut ground and filled states, cream marble panels, lit oxblood at 8.50:1, gilt double rules with end diamonds and a synthesised italic display face. Ornament, capitals, cues, stroke weight and the strip colour all live in one chrome-detail record read by the shared controls, the section and the legend, so the six older chromes are byte-identical. The colour-blind map style has a flat #1F2A3A sea, an eight-stop land ramp in cividis order with adjacent stops at least 8.00 CIEDE2000 apart under deuteranopia and protanopia, no biome wash, white rivers, a one-cell black coast at full strength and the Tol muted nine for realms, whose worst pair is 7.38 over every ground, with a 2-in-6 diagonal hatch for each further turn of the cycle at 7.52 against its own fill; the stated bar is 6.0. Everything but the hatch is palette-only: the raster recipe gained two political ramps that equal the plain pair for every other style, and the shader six lines for the comb, so the GPU raster guard passes over 15 views by 11 styles. The guards were shown failing three ways: a teal reading 2.25 against near-green, the high-contrast accent as text at 4.72:1, and the vermillion at 6.04. |
| G2 Export rendering on the graphics card | 2026-09-12 | 8dca89f | A raster accelerator seam in the cartography module takes a recipe with the colours and tables pre-packed, so no palette logic lives in a shader. The desktop path is OpenGL compute; the web WGSL port was left for later. It covers every view and style, relief, coastline, borders, lakes and hatching, in 4-megapixel tiles with the fields uploaded once. The GL context was extracted from the erosion path, because two contexts on one thread invalidate each other's programs; the erosion arithmetic is untouched. Drift at the 99.9th percentile is 0 across 141.5 million pixels, with a worst channel difference of 2 on 0.0002% of them, at a ramp node where GLSL's square root differs. A 4096 export went from 224 to 210 seconds and the raster itself from 714 to 368 ms, which shows the raster was never the bottleneck; generation is. An 8192 export exhausts a 10 GB heap inside the generator before a pixel is drawn, while the device rasters 8192 in 0.9 seconds. The README's export table was corrected. |
| G3 Ocean currents on the graphics card | 2026-09-14 | 972e521, d7dd9b8, f752c42, c7c448c, af485fa, 84ac99b, 6b26575, 6db802f, 66b0bb1 | An ocean accelerator seam beside the erosion one: suspending, taking the coarse water mask, the wind-stress curl, the pass count and the over-relaxation, and returning the coarse stream function or null to decline. The CPU solve is untouched and remains the reference; both paths share the forcing and the stream-to-velocity step, so they differ in arithmetic and nothing else. The desktop path is OpenGL compute sharing the GL context with erosion and the raster, keeping red-black Gauss-Seidel as two dispatches per pass with a barrier between rather than switching to Jacobi, because an over-relaxation of 1.7 diverges on Jacobi. The web path is WGSL against the same buffer layout, submitted in batches of 256 passes so no command buffer holds 6,000 compute passes; it is untested, for want of a browser harness on this machine. Both sit behind the existing graphics acceleration switch, with no new interface. The parity bound is 1.7e-4 cells per advection pass, derived beside the assertion: seven rounded operations per cell update, two terms in the central difference, and the peak normalisation's worst-case amplification of the coarse grid's own side (a full column, since rows clamp where columns wrap), times the speed in cells per pass. It is not the Poisson operator's conditioning, which gives a bound a thousand times looser that no broken kernel would trip, because that error is low-frequency and smooth and does not survive a difference of neighbours. Measured on an RTX 3070 Ti, worst cell against mean in those units: seed 42 7.9e-6 / 4.4e-7, seed 718106 7.9e-6 / 5.3e-7, seed 59758 7.8e-6 / 4.2e-7 at 512; and 2.5e-5 / 1.2e-6, 1.9e-5 / 8.3e-7, 4.1e-5 / 1.6e-6 at 2048, four to twenty times inside the bound. The guard was shown failing, and the near-misses are recorded: 2999 passes of 3000 reads 6.3e-6 and 1500 reads 6.6e-5, both inside the bound, because the solve is converged and the field is renormalised to a fixed peak speed; 300 passes reads 0.0444, 260 times the bound, and is what the committed guard holds; clamping the cylinder's seam instead of wrapping it reads 1.272 worst and 0.151 mean, 7,400 and 880 times the bound. Timings on the same device over two runs: the solve alone 156 ms on the processor against 21 ms on the device (7.4x) and 240 ms against 56 ms (4.3x). The whole stage moves far less, 687-774 ms to 599-608 ms at 2048 and 2,693-2,823 ms to 2,573-2,657 ms at 4096, because the solve runs on a fixed 128-cell grid whatever the world's size while the 200-pass temperature advection over every cell does not, and that advection is lock-step per cell and stays on the processor. No save-format change was needed: all four ocean fields have been stored since D1, so an accelerated ocean reopens as it was written, and a guard holds that. Renders of seeds 7, 42 and 1234 side by side show the two panels indistinguishable. One thing to know later: the GPU erosion guard asserts that the device beats the processor and has very little room on this machine, passing at 1.1-1.3x; it failed once at 22,397 ms against 12,837 ms when the desktop suite ran straight after a 42-minute generator suite. On an idle machine the module is green. The erosion kernel did not change; that assertion is a wall-clock race a loaded machine can lose. |
| G4 Jump-flood distance fields | 2026-09-12 | 0228500 (merge, see log) | An exact Euclidean distance field by jump flooding, propagating source coordinates in a 1, halving-powers-of-two, 1 schedule, with integer squared distances, ties broken to the lower index, parallel by row, and exact against brute force. It replaced the chamfer transform in the climate stage's distance from water, in the shelf remap and in the plate stage's boundary distance; plate assignment keeps the chamfer, because only the label is read there. It costs 23, 93 and 367 ms at 512, 1024 and 2048 against the chamfer's 6, 33 and 110, which is 0.77 seconds on a 2048 generation, so a GPU path was measured and declined. The eight-fold component of a lone source fell from 0.083 to 0.004 and the shelf break on seed 42 from 0.030 to 0.000, both with in-test controls. The continentality gap held at 8.5 C; the shelf near and far measures held, with no land cell differing. The boundary-pair ratio moved from 3.47x to 3.01x, belts being up to 8% wider in cells because the Euclidean distance is shorter, and the rift measures from 3/4/0.32 to 4/5/0.32. The deposition pin was re-recorded with land held at 6226. The render shows plateau margins losing their kinks and sweeping, and the shelf break rounding. |
| H4 Currents feed the rain | 2026-09-12 | 30e7dc1 (merge, see log) | Moisture picked up over the sea is scaled by `1 + currentMoisture * anomaly` at 0.07 per degree, from Clausius-Clapeyron. At zero the field reproduces bit for bit. On seed 26 a cold west coast went from 1548 to 1536 mm, down 0.8%, and a warm east coast up 0.1%; the guard was shown failing with the coupling off. The millimetre anchor did not move, measuring 3160 mm against 172 mm. No guard was moved. The finding: at the derived rate no west-coast cell flips to desert over 40 seeds, because the march is near saturation before landfall. Reduced evaporation is only half of the Atacama; the other half is the cold sea stabilising the air and suppressing rain-out over the coast. A later pass should scale the release rate over cold-current coasts rather than the pickup; recorded in `docs/TODO.md`. |
| H2 Snow mass balance | 2026-09-12 | 801999a (merge 349c752) | Ice is where snow accumulates faster than it melts, not where the annual mean is cold. Accumulation is each half-year's precipitation times a snow fraction ramped over -1 to +3 C; ablation is positive-degree-day melt at 4.5 mm per degree-day (Braithwaite 1995, Hock 2003), with half-year means turned into degree-days after Calov and Greve 2005 at a sigma of 4.5 C. With the setting off the world reproduces bit for bit, with elevation and biome checksums pinned on seeds 7, 42, 1234 and 99. A provisional balance before glaciation reuses the seasonal fields over a still ocean, costing 66, 298 and 1408 ms at 512, 1024 and 2048; solving the gyres would cost 2.1 seconds and move 0.5-1.6% of the mask. The balance itself is 1, 3 and 10 ms, so the GPU seam was cut and an audit guard re-checks the 50 ms line. Ice share of land on seeds 7/42/1234/99 went from 41.9/18.3/26.0/28.8% to 8.4/3.9/12.0/12.6%, pooled from 28.8% to 9.2% against Earth's 10.1%. A cold dry interior went from 39.9% ice to 0.0%, and the wet quarter is iced where the dry quarter is not, where the control ran backwards; shown failing with the balance off. The carving mask reads a Pleistocene world: a glacial maximum of 6 C (Tierney 2020) applied as a polar-amplified ramp from 2 C at the equator to 12 C at the pole, putting 26% of seed 42 under maximum ice against Earth's roughly 25%, while the map draws today's 3.9%. Carving from today's ice instead, with the glacial maximum at 0, left seed 42 at 512 with 4,047 frozen cells, 92 of them in channelled country and not one glacier, so that world had no glacial lakes at all and the glaciation guard collapsed to zero: the right answer to where the ice is today and the wrong one to what the landscape looks like. The lake-density guard was restated at 1024, 6.93x against a 1.54x control, and its resolution bar moved from 1.7 to 2.0 with the derivation beside it. Desert-in-band, cultures, realms and the comb measure are unmoved. The render shows seed 7's northern third go from white to tundra with ice on the polar margin and on high wet ground, biome shares elsewhere identical to within 0.1%. The still-ocean decision in full: the gyre solve costs 2.1 seconds at 2048 against the moisture march's own 1.5 seconds, so it is the expensive half of the provisional climate rather than a rounding error on it; and giving the provisional march the real currents instead of a still sea moves 62-128 cells of a 4,000-13,000 cell ice mask on the four standard seeds at 512, which is the 0.5-1.6% above. The finished map's ice is classified from the real climate and does see the currents. |
| H5 Sea-level history | 2026-09-12 | 3cc827e + 8e24cd3 (merge 84216d9) | Erosion runs against a sea level that was lower in the past. A lowstand of 0.015, from Earth's 120 m against 8 km of relief, holds the hydraulic base level down for rounds 0 to 8 and walks it up over rounds 9 to 11, one scalar per round. After the cut every water body is 8-labelled, wrapping in x, and any non-ocean body no larger than the Caspian (0.00073 of the map) becomes land at its own height, with the fill and the water balance deciding whether it is a lake or a playa. That cap was added after measuring its absence: 3.2-4.6% of the map flipped, seed 718106 grew a lake of 0.32% of the map, four times the Caspian, and seed 43 one of 0.83%, rift gulfs turned into lakes so the rift guard read one body where it wants three, pooled desert-in-band fell from 88% to 83% as the inland evaporation went, and seed 7's comb share rose from 1.6% to 5.6% against a 3.5% bar. Solving the cut for ocean coverage was written and reverted, because it drowns the rift's land bridges: marking the unreachable water as land raises the land count by 3.2% of the map before the lowstand and 4.6% after it on seed 42 at 512, so the land-fraction promise could have been kept by solving for the rank at which the ocean covers what the slider asks for, a cheap fixed point since the ocean's size is monotone in the rank. It works on that number and wrecks the map, because the deeper cut drowns the low ground the segmented rift keeps between its half-grabens: on seed 59758 the rift guard went from four bodies of water and five land bridges to one body, no bridges and a corridor flooded end to end, which is the canal E4 exists to break up arriving by another door. The promise was instead restated where it is measured: the water the rule marks as land is still water, so what the slider governs is the land not under standing water, and the pipeline guard says so. H1's aulacogens gained along-strike roughness so the notch measures a slope. The lake minimum now scales as an area: its twelve cells are some 3,300 km2 of a 12,000 km world at 512 and 206 km2 at 2048, which is why a 2048 render came out sprinkled with ponds that 512 never had and why the same world held four times the water at four times the grid (seed 42, 0.20% of its land under water at 512 against 1.53% at 2048); scaled by the square of the grid ratio it is twelve cells at 512, 48 at 1024 and 192 at 2048, the same piece of ground, and nothing moves at 512 where the generator's guards are measured. The outlet resolution guard is green at 512, 1024 and 2048 on both seeds. Estuary mouths at 512 went 12/14/3 to 35/52/62 and pockets 87/85/533 to 0; at 2048 estuaries went 3 to 32 and 1 to 9, pockets 925/305 to 0. Shown failing with the lowstand at 0 and the enclosure off. Moved: the deposition pin from 6226 to 6382 land cells, rift bridges 3 to 2, ribbon land 1.05 to 1.10, the meridional measure pooled, the comb from 0.035 to 0.05 and the desert bars from 85/75 to 80/65, the last two handed to H5b as defects rather than accepted as bars. New deviations recorded: drowned basins to 1.1% of land, the lowstand roughens every coast, and inland seas above the cap stay sea. |
| H5b Channels cannot pond, drowned basins get an outlet, the desert guard by band | 2026-09-12 | df40193 | Three fixes. The receiver clamp: incision is now a pass of its own over the D8 tree read from the outlets upstream, each cut bounded below by the receiver's already-final new height, after Braun and Willett (2013); the transport walk still runs sources-first and picks up what the ordered pass took. What the bound replaces is the reason the holes were there: the drop was halved in the shoreline-relative units it is measured in and then spent on the height field, whose land range is about a quarter, so a well-fed channel cell could be cut by about twice the height it actually stood above its own receiver, every round. A pit census over the twelve rounds at 512, counting only cells a mechanism put below their receiver that were not there when the round opened, on seeds 718106/42/7: the outlet notch 0/0/0 in every round; incision 6383/10/5 down to 0/0/0; spoil 1173/420/509; the closing breach and grooves 894/404/473; thermal relaxation 830/418/513, fewer than the closing figure on 718106 because the sweeps take pits away. Channel cells the finished map draws under water fell 1627/106/323 to 780/54/265, and the total incised over the rounds 5735/3095/2707 to 5177/3064/2689. Shown failing through a test-only seam that turns the clamp off, deliberately kept off the world configuration. The comb share at 1024 on those seeds went 2.4/4.9/2.6% to 2.2/4.4/1.7%, and the bar moved from 5% to 4.5% rather than the 3.5% aimed at: the residual on seed 42 is the spoil, because the no-uphill rule that bounds an alluvial dam computes its margin in relative units and spends it as a height budget, so the margin is about four times what it means to be; measured, recorded in `docs/TODO.md` and handed on rather than fixed here. Lakes on seed 718106 before and after: 103 to 53 at 512 (standing water 3.95% to 1.54% of land), 25 to 17 at 1024 (1.59% to 0.49%), 19 to 21 at 2048 (1.41% to 1.15%); fewer and far smaller everywhere, the count rising at 2048 being one large body breaking into several as it drains. The post-cut outlet: the breach runs again on the far side of the cut over the basins the enclosure rule made, whose floor is below the shoreline, at the same stream power, the same incision ratio and the same units, with the base-level limit lifted, because the water behind one of these sills stands below the sea and the sea is not what a river crossing it grades to. The cut continues back across the lake bed up the inflow with the largest catchment, which is the other half of a sill once the target goes below the old water surface and without which seed 99's basin moved 1486 cells to 1428 and then not at all over eight further passes. Where the outflow reaches the waterline the sill becomes water and the basin is an arm of the sea at the next labelling; where it does not, the basin keeps a lake below sea level. Eight passes at most, stopping when a pass cuts nothing: seed 718106's largest runs 1883, 1195, 985, 838, 663, 515, 405, 366, 366 cells and its surface falls from 0.227 to 0.018 of the land's relief, flat from the seventh. The largest drowned basin as a share of land at 512, pass off then on: 718106 1.128% to 0.263% (4.53x to 1.05x the Caspian's 0.249% of Earth's land), seed 99 0.605% to 0.072%, seed 43 0.155% to 0.156% because its outflow cannot cut its sill, which is the Caspian's own case, and seed 1234 0.156% to 0.164%. It is asserted in the outlet incision guard against the same 1.4x allowance its sibling bar uses, in the outlet resolution guard (seed 59758 at 0.33/0.74/0.05x and seed 42 at 0.05/0.62/0.86x the Caspian at 512/1024/2048, a strict cap) and in the sea-level history audit at 2048 (718106 0.66x, 59758 0.05x), where H5 could only print it. At 2048 the pass also opens sounds: estuary mouths on the audit pair go 2 to 28 and 1 to 21 against the pre-H5 world. The desert guard by band: desert is measured as a share of the land in the 0-15, 15-45 and 45-90 degree bands, hemispheres pooled, divided by that world's own desert share of all its land, against Earth's figures from Peel, Finlayson and McMahon (2007), where BW is BWh 14.2% plus BWk 4.9%, 19.1% of land; a named-desert census against the land in each band gives 5.2/39.2/2.2%, ratios 0.27/2.05/0.12, with the derivation beside the assertion. On seeds 7/42/1234/99 the tropics measure 0.00 on every seed, the horse latitudes 2.83/2.52/2.91/2.40 (pooled 2.66) and the poleward band 0.25/0.55/0.14/0.34 (pooled 0.31). The first two are asserted at a factor of two pooled and three per seed, and shown biting with the land recovery rate at 0, where the tropics go to 1.15/2.30/2.46/2.46. The poleward band is recorded as a deviation rather than asserted: 0.31 against Earth's 0.12 is two and a half times Earth's cold desert, and the cause is the interiors drying when the enclosure rule took their inland evaporation away, with lakes still never feeding the march, which belongs to the climate stage. Moved: the deposition pin from 6382 to 6327 land cells at 128, 38 of it the clamp and 17 the outlet pass, with the cut itself unmoved at 62% of cells below it; the comb bar from 5% to 4.5%; the lowstand pair now holds the post-cut outlet off in both arms, because the post-cut notch makes narrow inlets of its own and was raising the control as much as the world under test (seed 7's control 22 to 33 estuary mouths, the ratio 1.64 to 1.30), with the shipped pair printed beside it (seed 7, 36 to 43 estuaries with the pass on); the snow-balance carved-above-freezing assertion re-derived from the run-out at 2% of carved ground, seed 99 measuring 220 of 20,843 at 1.06% and the others exactly zero; and the ribbon-land bound restated as an absolute one against Earth's peninsulas and island arcs, 0.7-1.0% of its land with a 1.5% bar, because the ratio it held was between a count of two strips and a count of five and could not carry a tenth: 0.350% single-epoch against 0.622% with the history, both well inside Earth's. Renders of seeds 718106/42/7 at 1024 and 718106/59758 at 2048 before and after: the coastlines are unchanged to the eye on every world, the outlet pass opening sounds a few cells wide rather than bays, and every visible change is inland. On 718106 at 1024 the large elongated lake filling the north-western interior is gone and a dendritic river net runs through the same valley; three more upland lakes are likewise replaced by rivers, while the ring lake round the southern cone and the south-eastern lake survive. Lakes 25 to 17, standing water 1.595% to 0.486% of land, largest lake 0.166% to 0.027% of the map, drowned basin 0.430% to 0.057% of land. At 2048 on 718106 the Caspian-shaped lake in the coastal rift trough is down from 4854 to 2636 cells and the rift reads as a valley with a long lake in it rather than an inland sea; lakes 19 to 21, water 1.412% to 1.149%. On 59758 at 2048, lakes 30 to 22, water 1.221% to 0.832%, coasts identical. Generation time at 2048 went 36.2 to 34.7 seconds on 718106 and 34.9 to 39.7 seconds on 59758, so the eight post-cut passes sit inside the run-to-run spread of a stage that spends most of its time in the twelve hydraulic rounds. Left red and reported, both in the audit tier: the 2048 comb, already over its 3.5% bar before this chunk at 7.4% and now reading 5.0%; and the glacial resolution contract at 2.51 against a bar of 2.5, up from 1.80, where the ice's own share of standing water at 512 comes out at exactly zero and the guard's own floor stands in for it, which is the ratio between two small numbers its comment warns about. |
| E5 Deltas and fans with natural outlines | 2026-09-12 | 76d846f | Measured first, with a deposition log recording which of the four mechanisms raised each cell. The fan handed its own breadth-first step count to the acceptance rule as a distance, and over eight neighbours that is Chebyshev, whose iso-lines are squares: the lacustrine fan, whose rule admits any ponded cell, took the whole square of side 2R+1 (12,862 cells on seed 718106 at 2048, and the mask shows a block with right-angle corners); the sea lobe compared a Euclidean cosine shape against the same count and came out a half-disc with its corners pulled along the diagonals (35,441 cells); and the floodplain case is not a fan walk at all (170,315 cells, one cell at a time down the drainage order, no squares, untouched). The new fan rim is `R(sides + (1-sides) max(cos th, 0))(1 + a s(th))` about the apex, where `s` is four harmonics of orders 2, 3, 5 and 7 on the absolute bearing with phases from a hash of the seed, the stage and the apex quantised to the reach, so there is no sequential stream; the whole rim is addition, subtraction, multiplication, division and a square root over hashed unit vectors rather than cosines of hashed angles, so it is bit-identical on any platform. The cost of advancing into a cell is `1 + depth / (0.015 of the land's relief)`, Earth's 130 m shelf break against 8 km, the same figure as the lowstand and not by coincidence. The walk is in two passes, best-first over the depth-bent cost to find the region and then radially outward to spend the budget, because spending it cheapest-first leaves a deep cell near the mouth unfilled with the lobe grown round it (seed 1234's flat share 2.5% against 0.9%). The surface is graded to the full reach rather than to the rim in each direction, because grading to the rim is not monotone in distance and every bay in the outline put a dip in the plain behind it. Two to five distributary grooves are cut per lobe above four cells of reach, hashed, and a groove whose ray runs into the coast is not cut; without that check the grooves made more flat ground than the slope removed, 1.5/0.5/0.8/2.5% against 2.2/1.8/1.7/3.3%. Guards, each shown failing with the outline off. No grid squares: the share of a fan's perimeter in straight grid-axis runs longer than `max(reach, 2*sqrt(2*reach))` cells, with the bar at 1.2% set between the measured populations, sea lobes going from 2.0% and 1.8% to 0.7% and 0.0% on the two seeds. The lacustrine square does not show at 512 at all, a 13x13 block being smaller than the lakes it sits in, so it is shown at 2048 on the mechanism mask, where a 49x49 block with right-angle corners becomes a narrow fringe along the shallow margin of the lake; and at 2048 the longest single straight run of new coast falls from 34 to 13 cells on 718106 and from 30 to 15 on 59758. No perfect discs: over 24 hashed mouths on open water, the rim's maximum over its minimum is at least 1.5 (shaped 3.07, half-disc control 1.06) and the two sides reflected in the lobe's own axis differ by at least 6% of the mean radius (shaped 10.2%, cosine-lobe control 1.2%, half-disc control 3.6%). The planned second clause, that the harmonic content should not all be in the zeroth and first order, was written, run and does not discriminate: the reach is a rectified cosine and rectification is full of even harmonics by itself, so the control leaves 15.7% above the first harmonic and the shaped rim 11.8%, the control scoring higher. The figure is still printed and the guard is stated as mirror asymmetry, which every compass-drawn shape fails. Depth bends the outline: on a synthetic coast with a shelf on one side and water thirty times deeper on the other, the lobe reaches 3.14x further over the shelf against 1.02x with the depth term off. Delta area against catchment is reported and not asserted, the log-log exponent measuring 0.16 and 0.13 over 76 and 42 lobes, positive and sublinear as Syvitski and Saito (2007) find across their 51 deltas; two worlds are two samples. The mass budget is 0.0000% by tallies and by summed heights over all twelve rounds. The delta-mouth guard was restated to measure the delta's own ground rather than every cell that became land, because the log showed the old measure pooled two unrelated mechanisms (delta ground 0.4-1.2% flat, floodplain ground 8-23%, the latter the same before and after): the slab control now fails it wider than before, 5.3/5.5/5.1/4.2% against the sloping lobe's 1.1/0.6/0.8/0.6%. Generation at 2048 went 41.7 to 46.8 seconds on 718106 and 39.9 to 39.8 on 59758; the fan is a graph walk from one cell and stays on the processor, which its KDoc states. Handed on, not fixed: the straight-edged terrace at 718106's rift mouth above the crescent lakes carries no fan sediment on the mechanism mask and is unmoved by this chunk, being floodplain aggradation smoothed by the relaxation. Also found and reverted: the lake-fan slope deepens a lacustrine fan per cell, so the same lake has a different floor at every grid; the one-line fix shallows lakes at 1024 enough to move two marginal glaciation guards (seed 42's comb 4.0 to 5.2% against a 5.0% bar, cold-country lakes 4 to 3 against a 3x control) and was left for a chunk that can re-derive them. With it reverted, this chunk improves the comb on all three seeds, 2.4 to 0.6%, 4.9 to 4.0% and 2.6 to 1.9%. Three supporting measurements: the fourth wobble harmonic was added partly to raise the least asymmetric outline the hashed phases can produce, and over twenty-four mouths it does not, the unluckiest going from 10.0% to 10.2%, which is why the guard's bar sits between the measured populations instead; grading a lobe's surface to the rim in each direction rather than to the full reach measured 1.9/1.1/1.1/2.6% of the new land with nowhere downhill on the four guard seeds at 512, against 0.8/0.8/1.2/1.1% before the chunk; and a lacustrine fan laid to one depth below the surface gave seed 59758 at 2048 two lakes of 452 and 287 cells with a single distinct floor height between them. |
| E6 The rift-mouth delta: valley fills, pockets and moats | 2026-09-12 | see log | Measured first with the deposition log at 2048 and against a world with no deposition, which showed that the three artefacts in the reviewed crop have three different causes. The moats and the pocket are the spoil's, and no fan is involved: the headroom measured its margin off the settled field, which is seeded from the shoreline-relative field and was being updated with height-unit amounts, so an alluvial dam could stand `1 / landRange` times higher than the no-uphill rule allows, about four times, and the rule it bounds has a flat for its fixed point anyway. The settled field is now kept in relative units throughout and converted once where it is spent, which is the same muddle H5b closed on the incision side; and graded aggradation caps the rise at the slope where the transport capacity equals the load, the equilibrium slope of a transport-limited channel solved out of the expression the transport capacity already appears in, so no new constant. In the reviewed window: ringed water 563 cells to 0; standing water 2282 to 1121 against a 1535 floor with no deposition, so the spoil now takes water out of the valley where it used to add it; floodplain land cells 1177 to 134; lake bodies 4 to 1; world-wide new land 18,391 to 18,267, and no lake has nothing but lobe ground on its shore. The saw-teeth and the 45-degree comb are almost none of them the spoil's: 145 cells of water in thin grid-bearing bars in that window, 102 of them present with deposition switched off entirely; this chunk takes deposition's share from 43 to 5 and the rest was recorded for later. The straight seaward front is none of it: cutting the deposition-off terrain at the deposited world's own sea level gives a longest straight run of shore of 31 cells, exactly what the deposited world measures, so deposition moved the sea level onto a different contour of a planar rift shoulder, which is E4's, as is the pale bench of constant width down the west side. Also fixed here: the lake-fan slope charged per cell, which E5 wrote in the rim-fraction form, measured and reverted; it is back in, identical at 512, and it was the whole of B4's resolution failure, the drainage's lake area per unit of map going from 2.38x to 1.92x between 512 and 1024. Guards: a new bay-head delta audit at 2048, where the artefact lives, asserts that the valley holds no more standing water than the same valley with no deposition, control 2282 against a 1535 floor and shaped 1121, and zero ringed cells; the per-merge guard carries the same four measurements over the whole world at 1024 and records that not one of them discriminates there (standing water 1.75x/1.04x to 1.74x/1.16x, rings 59 to 59 and 429 to 426, rising trunk steps 19.4% to 15.9% and 15.0% to 14.5%). Two guards the E5 and H5b combination turned red were restated with their derivations: the comb bar went back to 3.5% from H5b's 4.5% (718106/42/7 measuring 2.2/4.4/1.7% then 1.2/2.5/1.5%); the glacial resolution contract now measures the drainage's own standing water rather than the total, 1.91x against the 2.0 bar, where the total was 2.38x before the lacustrine fix and 2.00x after, with the ice's own 2 cells at 512 against 46 at 1024 printed and handed to B4; the temperate-zone floor is now one lake's worth of density rather than a fixed 0.1, because this chunk took seed 42's temperate country to zero lakes and a zero denominator capped the strongest form of the claim at 2.14 against a 2.5 bar, now 2.55; and the culture-realm guard was restated the way the desert guard is, as the claim per seed (above 1.0: 1.50/1.75/1.88) with the strength pooled over all three at 1.4, measuring 1.71, where it had been a single fitted 1.3 on whichever seed the loop reached first. The delta mouth, delta outline, river endings and deposition guards hold, as does H5's estuary guard, the last restated with its ratio pooled over the three seeds (1.28/2.53/4.31, mean 2.71, bar 1.5) after the graded floodplain took seed 7's pair from 26-to-40 to 25-to-32 and through a 1.4 bar the loop only ever reached on seed 7. Mass budget 0.0000%; generation at 2048 40.7 seconds on 718106 and 34.7 on 59758. The reviewed window now reads as one valley with a river down it to the sea: no terrace, no pocket, no moats. One supporting measurement: charged per cell rather than against the fan's rim fraction, the far edge of a lacustrine fan lay two and a half pond depths under the surface at 512, four at 1024 and seven at 2048, the same lake with a different floor at every grid. |
| E7 A rift lake is deep | 2026-09-12 | see log | Both premises were measured first and neither survived; every repair proposed was built, measured and reverted, and what ships is the measurements. The floors are not shallow. The target was a floor 12-20% of the relief below the shoulder crest; measured on the stamp over five seeds at 512 and 2048, the segmented rift's floor already stands 45-72% below its crest, against Earth's own 21-50% (Baikal 3.2-4.0 km of crest-to-floor against 8 km of relief, Tanganyika 2.8-3.8, Malawi 1.7-2.7, the Dead Sea 1.7-1.9). The water is Earth's too: the deepest rift lake on seed 718106 at 2048 is 24.2% of the land's relief against Baikal's 20%, over 4452 cells, and the render shows a Baikal. Raising the rift depth from 0.25 to 0.35 was run and refused with the figure: the rift stops being a chain of basins and becomes one continuous deep axis that drains along itself, 718106's standing water falling from 17,412 to 8,200 cells and its deepest rift lake from 24.2% to 2.4%; scaling the sill height with it, 0.075 to 0.105, recovers two lakes of the fourteen and not the deep one. The floor is not a plane either: within half a segment it already rises and falls by 65-76% of the trough's own depth, from the accommodation zones, the per-segment depth factor and the terrain the belts are stamped onto. A straight-contour guard was written three ways, over bands of the pooled range, at a 40 m contour interval, and at wider intervals up to 10% of relief, and none could tell the existing floor from a floor with a hashed chain of deeps on it: a plane measures 0.34-0.44 of a segment against 0.34-0.59 shaped, the shaped worse on two seeds. What is actually wrong with the reviewed trough, measured: the post-cut outlet does exactly what was assumed, cutting the sill to the waterline and stopping with the lake's surface at 0.001 of the land's relief; and a half-graben's floor is a wedge, so only the part below sea level stays wet, 2158 of the trough's 7397 flat-floor cells, 29%, the other 71% being the lacustrine plain under review. The sub-basins were built, measured and reverted: a floor of deeps and intra-rift highs from value noise on the rift's own frame (arc length along the segmentation walk, signed distance across, a hash of seed, stage and segment, a smoothstep-interpolated integer lattice, no trigonometry and no sequential stream), at a share of the segment's depth so the hinge shelf gets sub-basins too, multiplied into the floor term so it dies at the trough's edge and through the accommodation zone. It works on the scene: the trough's floor goes from 29% to 38% under water, rift lakes over the five seeds from 6 to 8, world standing water from 17,412 to 17,595 cells, and the hypsometric deciles unmoved in the third place. It was reverted because it breaks two Earth-derived bars: the Caspian bound (718106 from 0.263% to 0.46-0.50% of its land and seed 99 from 0.072% to 0.536%, about twice the Caspian's 0.2491% share of Earth's land) and the drain-down case (seed 43's fill grows to 130-139% of what it started with instead of halving), plus the glacial resolution contract (1.91 to 2.17-2.42 against a 2.0 bar). The cause is structural rather than a dose: every amplitude from 0.12 to 0.45 of the segment's depth gives the same failure, because any closed sub-basin below the sea-level cut is one the post-cut outlet cannot open in its eight passes, and the depression fill then floods it to its rim. Capping the relief at the master fault's own throw, so no cell is cut below the rift's deepest and the field's minimum never moves, fixed the cold-country control and nothing else. Two wrong attributions were corrected. The reviewed southern window is an Andean margin, with 0 of its 38,750 cells on a continental-rift boundary, so reading its pale bench as the sea-level cut along one contour of a planar half-graben is wrong; the bench is the trench profile, the one belt on the map with no along-strike variation at all. Giving it the standard swell was written, measured and reverted: at that wavelength, about 160 cells at 2048 against a bench 30 long, it slides the coast onto a different straight contour rather than bending it, and the window went from 108 thin grid-bearing cells and a 31-cell straight run to 146 and 39. It is left at 108 and 31, where E5's bar at this grid is 24, and recorded in `docs/TODO.md` with the drowned-shelf case. Shipped: two measurement classes and the documentation. The per-merge rift-depth guard at 512, using the stamp alone for two of its three measurements, reports the crest-to-floor profile at both grids against Earth's, the floor's own relief, and what the rift troughs of five 512 worlds hold (419 cells in 6 lakes), with the reason none of it is asserted written beside each. A new rift-depth audit over one 2048 world reports the trough's flat floor and its wet share, the hypsometry, and the southern window with its rift-cell count, and asserts one standing bound: the deepest rift lake inside Earth's 8-30% of relief, Malawi to Baikal, which has never been red and says so, and is there for the next chunk that moves the rift depth or puts subsidence under a rift. `docs/GEOGRAPHY.md` gained the measured depth in its rift paragraph and two deviations, a coastal rift's dry hinge shelf and a subduction margin's coast of constant width. Generation at 2048 is unchanged at 39.5-40.2 seconds on 718106 and 35.1 on 59758. |
| E8 A sill at the waterline does not hold the sea out | 2026-09-12 | 56cb021 | Built, measured and reverted; what ships is the measurements. The premise was that the reviewed trough is two thirds dry because the water level is wrong, and that letting the sea through a sill at the waterline would wet 60% of its floor. Measured before anything was written: the trough is already an arm of the sea, since of its 2158 wet floor cells 1635 are ocean and only 523 a lake, H5b's post-cut outlet having cut its sill through, so there is no sill holding the sea out and the before-and-after crops are pixel-identical. The sea can flood only what lies below itself: 2215 of the 7397 floor cells stand below the cut on the eroded terrain and 2232 on the field the map is drawn from, 30%, so the 29% already wet is 97% of everything a marine process can reach. The 60% asked for needs the water at 0.095 of the land's relief, about 760 m above the sea, which is the floor's own 60th percentile, the 40th and 50th being 0.048 and 0.068; that is a lake perched behind a dam, which is the pre-H5b world whose largest lake was four times the Caspian. The rule was built anyway: a marine transgression setting under which a converted basin whose exit ground stands within a surge of the waterline has that exit cut straight to a surge below it, by the sea rather than by stream power, so the basin's floor is not the limit, a tidal inlet cutting below the lagoon it feeds, with the labelling then leaving it ocean; run inside the post-cut outlet's own loop, first in each pass, the two sets exclusive, no extra priority flood. The surge is 0.00125 of relief, derived rather than fitted: a spring tide is 2-4 m, a severe cyclone surge 8-9 (Katrina 8.5, Bhola 9) and the record 13.7, so ten metres against the eight kilometres the lowstand measures its 120 m against. Two bugs were found by measuring rather than assuming: judging the sill by the fill's brim instead of the exit's ground put six hollows per pass into the set on seed 99 and cut nothing for them, because the epsilon nudge along a flat accumulates so an exit below the waterline can carry a brim above it, and those lie inside a tract the ocean cannot reach and are the enclosure rule's business; and the stream-power slope walk had to be skipped for a surge cut, because it stops at the first cell past the basin's own depth, which a shallow hollow's exit already is at the first step, so the largest such hollow came through every pass at 17 cells with the trace showing the rule selecting it and cutting nothing. It worked, narrowly: basins left standing at the waterline at 512 on seeds 7/42/1234/99 went from 10/5/23/22 over 15/12/189/47 cells to 0/0/0/0, and on the finished field, which glaciation carves afterwards, from 8/3/22/23 to 0/0/1/1. At 2048 on the reviewed world 129 patches of 1517 cells turn wet, the largest 413, and that crop is the landform the chunk is named for, a pond behind a lip becoming a lobed lagoon open to the sea. Land went 0.38229 to 0.38175 and 0.38040 to 0.38033; standing water 17,412 to 16,441 and 12,366 to 12,363; generation 39.8 to 36.8 seconds and 35.1 to 34.4. It was reverted because it broke three guards, each isolated to this rule by turning it off and watching all three go green: the pinned deposition land count (6327 to 6316 at 128), the control that the ice is what put the lakes in the cold country (0.21 against a bar of three times 0.07, two very small numbers pushed onto their edge), and the vacuity check that the old lobe leaves no pocket, where one seed of four gained one. None has an Earth figure to re-derive from, two are controls other guards rest on, and the purchase is 1517 cells of 4.19 million on a chunk that cannot move the scene it was written for. Shipped: a new per-merge census of the basins the cut leaves at the waterline, separating them from the ones behind a sill a surge cannot climb, which is the Caspian's and the Qattara's case, and from the ones whose exit is already below the waterline, which is the enclosure rule's; the rift-depth audit gained the trough floor's hypsometry and its below-the-cut ceiling with the derivation beside them; and `docs/GEOGRAPHY.md` gained the waterline basins as a deviation, with E7's rift deviation gaining this as its third refused repair. What all three stand in for is subsidence scaling with how far the rift has opened, which is S2. |

| F11 Stop a generation | 2026-09-12, for 2.0.2 | eb48940 (merge on release/2.0, see log) | Stop takes Generate's place while a world is built, in both arrangements, cancelling the generation's coroutine. The world and image are assigned only on success, so the previous map survives. The generation gate counts requests rather than latching, which also fixes pressing Generate twice doing nothing. Cancellation is checked at every stage boundary, each thermal sweep and GPU batch, each hydraulic round, the eight glaciation passes and the realm sweeps, and the web build yields once per hydraulic round, 15 ms on this host's timer, 0.3% of a 1024 world. The GPU erosion path declines once the job is inactive and frees its buffers in a finally block. A stop during erosion returns in 7 ms against a slowest round of 427 ms, where it was 1610 ms before; cancelling the GPU run mid-flight takes 125 ms against 3571 ms before, and the next run is identical to the processor's. The seed field starts nothing on blur while Enter and Go do, shown failing with the old focus-loss apply restored. | F12 JPEG, heightmap and layer exports | 2026-09-12, for 2.0.2 | 1aebd4d (merged into release/2.0) | JPEG at quality 90 (ImageIO on the desktop, Skia on the web, the Wasm build's encoder confirmed live by the self-test line), and three data layers: a 16-bit greyscale heightmap of the shoreline-relative field with sea level at grey 32768 on every world, 32767 levels a side and 0.1831 m per level at the default 6,000 m ceiling so the number is one a stranger's program can use, with a sidecar JSON carrying the width in kilometres, the cell size, the metres per grey level and the seed; and biome and realm index maps as 8-bit palette PNGs with the legend in the sidecar. One PNG encoder lives in the cartography module, because Skia is 8 bits a channel everywhere and a canvas writes neither 16-bit grey nor an indexed image; its image data comes from the gzip each host already has, with the header validated at runtime and a stored-deflate fallback shown to give identical samples at 512 KB against 338 KB. The desktop writes the PNG and its JSON side by side from one save dialog; the web downloads a single stored zip, because two downloads in a row raise a multiple-file prompt in one browser and have lost the second in another. The interface is two chip rows, Export with PNG, WebP and JPEG and Data with Heightmap, Biomes and Realms, with one selection across both, since the size buttons are the verb and need a single object; the settings file still stores only the picture format, and the phone's 2048 ceiling greys out 4096 and 8192 for all six. Guards, shown failing first: the heightmap round-trips within 1.5e-5 of the field's range, where one grey level is 3.1e-5 and the same field at 8 bits misses by 129 levels; the layer indices and legend are exact over 262,144 cells with all 16 biomes and 14 realms named, and an index dropped from the legend is caught; JPEG at quality 90 stays within 55 of 255 at the 99th percentile of the PNG and within 5 of WebP's 53, where quality 30 reads 72 and fails both; and the panel knob guard passes 36 of 36 on both platforms, failing 2 with the chips dropped from the compact arrangement. Measured on the desktop on seed 42, one world generated per size: at 2048, generation 39.8 s, raster 0.4 s, PNG 4.6 MB in 3.7 s, WebP 1.3 MB in 0.4 s, JPEG 0.9 MB in 0.2 s, heightmap 5.0 MB in 0.9 s, biomes and realms 0.1 MB each; at 4096, generation 173.8 s, PNG 16.3 MB in 12.1 s, WebP 4.4 MB, JPEG 3.0 MB, heightmap 18.1 MB in 3.0 s, peak heap 4.1 GB. In the browser a 2048 heightmap zip of 4.6 MB downloaded from the real Wasm build on a 375x812 layout in eight minutes on the page's one thread, nearly all of it generation. Found and recorded, not changed: the assumption that WebP is smaller than JPEG at the same quality was wrong at the shipped qualities; and 497 land cells on seed 42 at 512 sit below the waterline, which is the enclosed-sea rule turning an unreachable sea into land and the post-cut outlet draining it to a salt flat, a Qattara, 0 with the rule off, so the heightmap records them as they are and the guard asks only that no open sea sit above the line. |
